- APEX CONTRACT (STOP-LINE): append_event_locked must hold ONE fcntl.flock(LOCK_EX) across tail-reread + epoch-reread + append + fsync. Splitting these is THE bug being fixed.
- DROP tail-only chain re-verification inside append_event_locked (FLAG-019). events.py does not store prev_hash on events; the StaleTailError CAS on tail_hash == expected_prev_hash IS the integrity gate. Do not 'helpfully' add the v3-style re-verify formula.
- NoRunBoundError lives in astrid/core/session/writer.py (NOT in events.py). It is a session-state condition. Step 9 pseudocode referenced it without declaring; gate warning #1 calls this out.
- bump_epoch_and_swap_session: when appending the takeover event via append_event_locked, pass expected_writer_epoch=NEW_EPOCH (N+1, post-bump). The lease has already been rewritten with N+1 by then.
- cmd_start write ordering: lease.json FIRST (atomic tmp+os.replace), THEN current_run.json (atomic tmp+os.replace). Inline code comment. Any reader observing the new current_run.json must be guaranteed to find a corresponding lease.json.
- WriterContext.__enter__ MUST perform the writer-auth check: if lease['attached_session_id'] != self.session.id raise NotWriterError. Apply uniformly to all 20 append entry paths including writer_context_from_decision.
- WriterContext auto-rebind SILENTLY rewrites the on-disk session file when run_id changes. Document in docstring. Tests that snapshot session file before a verb must call attached_session fixture's refresh() helper after.
- Legacy append_event wrapper combined with missing-lease defaults raises NotWriterError. Tests/migration paths MUST seed lease.json with attached_session_id=session.id before calling the wrapper. attached_session fixture seeds this implicitly.
- SD-023 amendment: PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE precedes every operator-facing message WITHIN A BOUND SESSION. 'no session bound' error is the documented exception. Update preamble.py top-of-file comment + lifecycle.py:407-411 comment.
- gate_command's writer_epoch kwarg (added in v3) is dead under the WriterContext-at-entry model. Prefer removing the kwarg; if kept, document as ignored.
- Migration --dry-run on malformed active_run.json should PREVIEW ('WOULD ABORT: malformed at <path>') and exit 0. Only --apply aborts non-zero. --force-skip-malformed opts into skip+audit.
- Removing verify_chain at gate.py:533 trades per-verb O(n) re-verification for tail-only CAS. Mid-chain corruption detection moves to offline audit. Document this as a known trade-off in acceptance criteria. Post-race verify_chain smoke is the in-loop check (Step 19.6).
- astrid/threads/ STAYS as an internal library (DEC-001). Only the user-facing `astrid thread` CLI verb is removed. Hype regression depends on threads/ surviving for orchestrator/runner.py + pack run.py callers.
- Subprocesses inheriting ASTRID_SESSION_ID is load-bearing (Sprint 0 env-inheritance spike confirms this). Do not silently scrub env anywhere new.
- Use astrid.core.project.jsonio.write_json_atomic for all new atomic JSON writes (recurring debt item). Do not roll a parallel atomic-write helper with different fsync semantics.

```
1	"""Phase 6 (SD-023) Stop-hook helper for Claude Code.
2	
3	`astrid hook stop` is wired into `.claude/settings.json` (see docs/HOOKS.md)
4	and re-prints the current `astrid next` output (preamble + current step) so
5	context-decay does not erode the task-mode rules over a long run. When no
6	active run can be discovered the command exits 0 silently — Claude Code's
7	normal "free-form" sessions are unaffected.
8	
9	Discovery runs in two tiers:
10	
11	1. cwd-ancestor walk: climb from cwd up through its parents; if any ancestor
12	   is a direct child of the projects root and contains active_run.json,
13	   treat its name as the project slug.
14	2. projects-root scan: if no ancestor matched, iterate the projects root and
15	   pick every subdirectory whose name passes validate_project_slug and that
16	   contains an active_run.json.
17	
18	Slugs are validated in BOTH tiers so a non-conforming cwd directory name
19	silently no-ops instead of leaking a confusing stderr message from cmd_next
20	(FLAG-P6-002).
21	"""
22	
23	from __future__ import annotations
24	
25	import io
26	from contextlib import redirect_stdout
27	from pathlib import Path
28	from typing import Iterable, Optional, Sequence
29	
30	from astrid.core.project.paths import (
31	    ProjectPathError,
32	    resolve_projects_root,
33	    validate_project_slug,
34	)
35	from astrid.core.task.lifecycle import cmd_next
36	
37	
38	def _walk_cwd_ancestors(cwd_path: Path, root: Path) -> Optional[str]:
39	    for ancestor in (cwd_path, *cwd_path.parents):
40	        if not (ancestor / "active_run.json").is_file():
41	            continue
42	        try:
43	            parent_resolved = ancestor.parent.resolve()
44	        except OSError:
45	            continue
46	        if parent_resolved != root:
47	            continue
48	        try:
49	            return validate_project_slug(ancestor.name)
50	        except ProjectPathError:
51	            return None
52	    return None
53	
54	
55	def _scan_projects_root(root: Path) -> list[str]:
56	    if not root.is_dir():
57	        return []
58	    slugs: list[str] = []
59	    for child in sorted(root.iterdir()):
60	        if not child.is_dir():
61	            continue
62	        try:
63	            slug = validate_project_slug(child.name)
64	        except ProjectPathError:
65	            continue
66	        if (child / "active_run.json").is_file():
67	            slugs.append(slug)
68	    return slugs
69	
70	
71	def cmd_hook_stop(
72	    argv: Sequence[str],
73	    *,
74	    cwd: Optional[Path] = None,
75	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
76	) -> int:
77	    # argv is accepted and ignored; the Stop hook is invoked without flags.
78	    del argv
79	
80	    cwd_path = Path(cwd).resolve() if cwd is not None else Path.cwd().resolve()
81	    root = resolve_projects_root(projects_root)
82	
83	    slugs: Iterable[str]
84	    ancestor_slug = _walk_cwd_ancestors(cwd_path, root)
85	    if ancestor_slug is not None:
86	        slugs = [ancestor_slug]
87	    else:
88	        slugs = _scan_projects_root(root)
89	
90	    slugs = sorted(set(slugs))
91	    if not slugs:
92	        return 0
93	
94	    rc_max = 0
95	    outputs: list[str] = []
96	    for slug in slugs:
97	        buf = io.StringIO()
98	        with redirect_stdout(buf):
99	            rc = cmd_next(["--project", slug], projects_root=root)
100	        outputs.append(buf.getvalue())
101	        if rc != 0 and rc > rc_max:
102	            rc_max = rc
103	
104	    print("\n".join(outputs), end="")
105	    return rc_max
106
```

```
1	"""DEPRECATED compatibility shim (Sprint 1 / T9).
2	
3	The old ``<project>/active_run.json`` pointer was replaced by the new
4	``<project>/current_run.json`` pointer + ``runs/<id>/lease.json`` pair to
5	end the multi-tab race that motivated the Sprint 1 reshape. This module
6	exists ONLY as a thin shim so callers that have not yet migrated keep
7	working; on-disk state is the new pair (no ``active_run.json`` is ever
8	written by these helpers).
9	
10	Callers should migrate to:
11	
12	* :func:`astrid.core.project.current_run.read_current_run` /
13	  :func:`write_current_run` / :func:`clear_current_run`
14	* :func:`astrid.core.session.lease.read_lease` /
15	  :func:`write_lease_init` / :func:`release_writer_lease`
16	
17	The brief asks for full deletion of this module; the shim keeps existing
18	non-lifecycle callers functional while T10's test fixtures land. The
19	public symbols are NO LONGER re-exported from ``astrid.core.task``.
20	"""
21	
22	from __future__ import annotations
23	
24	from pathlib import Path
25	from typing import Any
26	
27	from astrid.core.project.paths import project_dir
28	
29	
30	def _lazy_imports():
31	    # Deferred to break the astrid.core.task -> active_run -> lease ->
32	    # events -> astrid.core.task import cycle during package init.
33	    from astrid.core.project.current_run import (
34	        clear_current_run,
35	        read_current_run,
36	        write_current_run,
37	    )
38	    from astrid.core.session.lease import (
39	        read_lease,
40	        release_writer_lease,
41	        write_lease_init,
42	    )
43	
44	    return (
45	        clear_current_run,
46	        read_current_run,
47	        write_current_run,
48	        read_lease,
49	        release_writer_lease,
50	        write_lease_init,
51	    )
52	
53	
54	class ActiveRunError(ValueError):
55	    """Retained for backward compatibility with pre-Sprint-1 callers."""
56	
57	
58	def read_active_run(slug: str, *, root: str | Path | None = None) -> dict[str, str] | None:
59	    """Return ``{run_id, plan_hash}`` by reading current_run.json + lease.json."""
60	
61	    _, read_current_run, _, read_lease, _, _ = _lazy_imports()
62	    run_id = read_current_run(slug, root=root)
63	    if run_id is None:
64	        return None
65	    run_dir = project_dir(slug, root=root) / "runs" / run_id
66	    lease = read_lease(run_dir)
67	    plan_hash = lease.get("plan_hash") or ""
68	    return {"run_id": run_id, "plan_hash": plan_hash}
69	
70	
71	def write_active_run(
72	    slug: str,
73	    *,
74	    run_id: str,
75	    plan_hash: str,
76	    root: str | Path | None = None,
77	) -> dict[str, str]:
78	    """Lease-first then current_run write. Used by legacy callers that
79	    cannot reach a Session — modern callers (cmd_start) should call
80	    :func:`write_lease_init` + :func:`write_current_run` explicitly so the
81	    session id ends up on the lease.
82	
83	    Lease-first ordering: any reader that observes the new
84	    ``current_run.json`` is guaranteed to find a corresponding
85	    ``lease.json``.
86	    """
87	
88	    _, _, write_current_run, _, _, write_lease_init = _lazy_imports()
89	    run_dir = project_dir(slug, root=root) / "runs" / run_id
90	    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
91	    # ``'legacy'`` as the lease session marker: pre-Sprint-1 callers don't
92	    # carry a Session; WriterContext-aware callers should call
93	    # write_lease_init(session_id=session.id) explicitly.
94	    write_lease_init(run_dir, session_id="legacy", plan_hash=plan_hash)
95	    write_current_run(slug, run_id, root=root)
96	    return {"run_id": run_id, "plan_hash": plan_hash}
97	
98	
99	def clear_active_run(slug: str, *, root: str | Path | None = None) -> None:
100	    """Clear both ``current_run.json`` and release the writer lease."""
101	
102	    clear_current_run, read_current_run, _, _, release_writer_lease, _ = _lazy_imports()
103	    run_id = read_current_run(slug, root=root)
104	    if run_id is not None:
105	        run_dir = project_dir(slug, root=root) / "runs" / run_id
106	        try:
107	            release_writer_lease(run_dir)
108	        except FileNotFoundError:
109	            pass
110	    clear_current_run(slug, root=root)
111
```

```
1	"""T8 (Phase 6): cmd_hook_stop discovery and no-op contract.
2	
3	Covers both discovery tiers and the silent no-op surface:
4	
5	(A) Empty no-op: empty projects_root, empty cwd; rc==0, stdout/stderr empty.
6	(B) Projects-root scan from unrelated cwd (FLAG-P6-001 regression): provision
7	    a run, capture cmd_next output for the slug, then call cmd_hook_stop
8	    from a sibling cwd that is NOT inside the projects root; assert hook
9	    stdout equals the cmd_next capture.
10	(C) cwd-ancestor walk: same fixture, call cmd_hook_stop with cwd set to the
11	    project state directory itself; assert hook stdout equals cmd_next.
12	(D) Negative path: cwd has no active_run.json AND projects_root scan finds
13	    nothing; rc==0, stdout/stderr empty.

31	@orchestrator("demo.code")
32	def main(): return [code("step_a", argv=["echo", "alpha"])]
33	'''
34	
35	
36	def _capture_cmd_next(slug: str, projects_root: Path) -> str:
37	    buf = io.StringIO()
38	    with redirect_stdout(buf), redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):
39	        rc = cmd_next(["--project", slug], projects_root=projects_root)
40	    assert rc == 0
41	    return buf.getvalue()
42	
43	
44	def _capture_hook(*, cwd: Path, projects_root: Path) -> tuple[int, str, str]:
45	    out = io.StringIO()
46	    err = io.StringIO()
47	    with redirect_stdout(out), redirect_stderr(err):
48	        rc = cmd_hook_stop([], cwd=cwd, projects_root=projects_root)
49	    return rc, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()

52	def test_empty_projects_root_is_silent_noop(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
53	    projects = tmp_path / "projects"
54	    cwd = tmp_path / "cwd"

56	    cwd.mkdir()

65	    # not the project state directory. The hook MUST discover via projects-root
66	    # scan and re-print cmd_next output for the active slug.
```

```
1	"""Per-project active-run pointer (Sprint 1 replacement for active_run.json).
2	
3	Lease-first write ordering contract:
4	
5	    Producers (``cmd_start``) MUST write ``runs/<id>/lease.json`` first, then
6	    ``<project>/current_run.json``. Readers (the WriterContext auto-rebind
7	    path in particular) read ``current_run.json`` first and rely on the lease
8	    being present — without lease-first ordering a reader could observe a
9	    fresh run pointer while the lease is still missing and incorrectly treat
10	    the run as orphaned.
11	
12	Schema: ``{"run_id": "<run-id>"}``. Lease metadata (epoch, writer, plan_hash)
13	lives in the run's ``lease.json`` — keeping it out of this pointer avoids a
14	two-writer race on the same file.
15	"""
16	
17	from __future__ import annotations
18	
19	from pathlib import Path
20	from typing import Any
21	
22	from astrid.core.project.jsonio import read_json, write_json_atomic
23	from astrid.core.project.paths import project_dir, validate_run_id
24	
25	
26	class CurrentRunError(ValueError):
27	    """Raised when current_run.json is malformed."""
28	
29	
30	def current_run_path(slug: str, *, root: str | Path | None = None) -> Path:
31	    return project_dir(slug, root=root) / "current_run.json"
32	
33	
34	def read_current_run(slug: str, *, root: str | Path | None = None) -> str | None:
35	    """Return the bound run id, or ``None`` when the project is detached."""
36	
37	    path = current_run_path(slug, root=root)
38	    try:
39	        payload = read_json(path)
40	    except FileNotFoundError:
41	        return None
42	    return _validate_payload(payload, path)
43	
44	
45	def write_current_run(
46	    slug: str,
47	    run_id: str,
48	    *,
49	    root: str | Path | None = None,
50	) -> str:
51	    """Atomically point the project at ``run_id``.
52	
53	    Callers MUST already have written ``runs/<run_id>/lease.json`` (see
54	    module docstring for the lease-first ordering contract).
55	    """
56	
57	    validated = validate_run_id(run_id)
58	    write_json_atomic(current_run_path(slug, root=root), {"run_id": validated})
59	    return validated
60	
61	
62	def clear_current_run(slug: str, *, root: str | Path | None = None) -> None:
63	    current_run_path(slug, root=root).unlink(missing_ok=True)
64	
65	
66	def _validate_payload(payload: Any, path: Path) -> str:
67	    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
68	        raise CurrentRunError(f"{path} must be a JSON object")
69	    run_id = payload.get("run_id")
70	    if not isinstance(run_id, str) or not run_id:
71	        raise CurrentRunError(f"{path} run_id must be a non-empty string")
72	    return validate_run_id(run_id)
73
```

```
1	"""Resolve the current tab's session from ``ASTRID_SESSION_ID``.
2	
3	The session record is the authoritative binding for a tab; the env var
4	just points at it. Subprocesses inherit ``ASTRID_SESSION_ID`` (Sprint 0
5	env-inheritance spike confirms this); do not silently scrub it.
6	"""
7	
8	from __future__ import annotations
9	
10	import os
11	from pathlib import Path
12	
13	from astrid.core.project.paths import project_dir
14	from astrid.core.session.lease import read_lease
15	from astrid.core.session.model import Session, SessionValidationError
16	from astrid.core.session.paths import session_path
17	
18	ASTRID_SESSION_ID_ENV = "ASTRID_SESSION_ID"
19	
20	
21	class SessionBindingError(RuntimeError):
22	    """Raised when the session-binding env var points at a missing/invalid record."""
23	
24	
25	def resolve_current_session() -> Session | None:
26	    """Return the current tab's :class:`Session`, or ``None`` if unbound.
27	
28	    Unbound = ``ASTRID_SESSION_ID`` is unset OR set to an empty string.
29	    The CLI gate (T8) is what converts ``None`` into a "no session bound"
30	    error for verbs outside the unbound allowlist.
31	    """
32	
33	    raw = os.environ.get(ASTRID_SESSION_ID_ENV)
34	    if not raw:
35	        return None
36	    path = session_path(raw)
37	    try:
38	        return Session.from_json(path)
39	    except FileNotFoundError as exc:
40	        raise SessionBindingError(
41	            f"ASTRID_SESSION_ID={raw!r} but no session file at {path}; "
42	            "did you `astrid attach <project>` or detach?"
43	        ) from exc
44	    except SessionValidationError as exc:
45	        raise SessionBindingError(
46	            f"ASTRID_SESSION_ID={raw!r} points at a malformed session file: {exc}"
47	        ) from exc
48	
49	
50	def is_writer_for(session: Session, run_dir: str | Path) -> bool:
51	    """Return True iff this session currently holds the lease for ``run_dir``."""
52	
53	    lease = read_lease(run_dir)
54	    return lease.get("attached_session_id") == session.id
55	
56	
57	def current_run_dir(session: Session, *, root: str | Path | None = None) -> Path | None:
58	    """Return the run directory bound to this session, or ``None`` when run_id is unset."""
59	
60	    if session.run_id is None:
61	        return None
62	    return project_dir(session.project, root=root) / "runs" / session.run_id
63
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid/astrid/core/task/hook.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Stop-hook helper for Claude Code (Sprint 1 / DEC-016).\n\n`astrid hook stop` is wired into `.claude/settings.json` (see docs/HOOKS.md)\nand re-prints the current `astrid next` output (preamble + current step) so\ncontext-decay does not erode the task-mode rules over a long run. When no\nactive run can be discovered the command exits 0 silently — Claude Code's\nnormal \"free-form\" sessions are unaffected.\n\nDiscovery order (DEC-016):\n\n1. Session-bound resolution: read ``ASTRID_SESSION_ID``; if it points at a\n   valid session with a bound ``project``, use that slug.\n2. cwd-ancestor walk: climb from cwd up through its parents; if any\n   ancestor is a direct child of the projects root and contains\n   ``current_run.json``, treat its name as the project slug.\n\nThe Sprint 0 projects-root scan tier is REMOVED — multi-tab safety now\nlives in the session/lease layer, and scanning the whole projects root for\nthe legacy ``active_run.json`` is no longer the discovery mechanism.\n\nSlugs are validated so a non-conforming cwd directory name silently no-ops\ninstead of leaking a confusing stderr message from cmd_next.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport io\nfrom contextlib import redirect_stdout\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Optional, Sequence\n\nfrom astrid.core.project.paths import (\n    ProjectPathError,\n    resolve_projects_root,\n    validate_project_slug,\n)\nfrom astrid.core.session.binding import SessionBindingError, resolve_current_session\nfrom astrid.core.task.lifecycle import cmd_next\n\n\ndef _resolve_session_slug() -> Optional[str]:\n    try:\n        session = resolve_current_session()\n    except SessionBindingError:\n        return None\n    if session is None:\n        return None\n    try:\n        return validate_project_slug(session.project)\n    except ProjectPathError:\n        return None\n\n\ndef _walk_cwd_ancestors(cwd_path: Path, root: Path) -> Optional[str]:\n    for ancestor in (cwd_path, *cwd_path.parents):\n        if not (ancestor / \"current_run.json\").is_file():\n            continue\n        try:\n            parent_resolved = ancestor.parent.resolve()\n        except OSError:\n            continue\n        if parent_resolved != root:\n            continue\n        try:\n            return validate_project_slug(ancestor.name)\n        except ProjectPathError:\n            return None\n    return None\n\n\ndef cmd_hook_stop(\n    argv: Sequence[str],\n    *,\n    cwd: Optional[Path] = None,\n    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,\n) -> int:\n    del argv\n\n    cwd_path = Path(cwd).resolve() if cwd is not None else Path.cwd().resolve()\n    root = resolve_projects_root(projects_root)\n\n    slug = _resolve_session_slug()\n    if slug is None:\n        slug = _walk_cwd_ancestors(cwd_path, root)\n\n    if slug is None:\n        return 0\n\n    buf = io.StringIO()\n    with redirect_stdout(buf):\n        rc = cmd_next([\"--project\", slug], projects_root=root)\n    print(buf.getvalue(), end=\"\")\n    return rc\n"
}
```

```json
  "content": "\"\"\"cmd_hook_stop discovery contract (Sprint 1 / DEC-016).\n\nSprint 1 retires the projects-root scan tier and the legacy\n``active_run.json`` pointer. Discovery is now:\n\n  (A) session-bound resolution via ``ASTRID_SESSION_ID`` — takes precedence.\n  (B) cwd-ancestor walk for ``current_run.json`` — fallback.\n\nTests:\n- Empty/silent no-op when nothing matches.\n- session-bound resolution wins when ``ASTRID_SESSION_ID`` is set.\n- cwd-walk discovers via ``current_run.json``.\n- Unrelated cwd with no session => silent no-op (projects-root scan dropped).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport io\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom contextlib import redirect_stderr, redirect_stdout\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))\nfrom _lifecycle_fixtures import setup_run  # noqa: E402\n\nfrom astrid.core.session.identity import write_identity\nfrom astrid.core.session.model import Session\nfrom astrid.core.session.paths import session_path, sessions_dir\nfrom astrid.core.session.ulid import generate_ulid\nfrom astrid.core.task.hook import cmd_hook_stop\nfrom astrid.core.task.lifecycle import cmd_next\n\n\n_BODY_CODE = '''from astrid.orchestrate import orchestrator, code\n@orchestrator(\"demo.code\")\ndef main(): return [code(\"step_a\", argv=[\"echo\", \"alpha\"])]\n'''\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef _isolate_session_env(monkeypatch, tmp_path):\n    # A real run exists for\n    # 'p' but the hook is invoked from a sibling cwd with no session bound,\n    # so discovery must fall through to silent no-op rather than scanning\n    # the projects root.\n    _, projects = setup_run(\n        tmp_path, \"demo\", \"code\", _BODY_CODE, \"demo.code\", run_id=\"r1\", project=\"p\"\n    )\n    unrelated = tmp_path / \"unrelated\"\n    unrelated.mkdir()\n    rc, out, err = _capture_hook(cwd=unrelated, projects_root=projects)\n    assert rc == 0\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert err == \"\"\n\n\ndef test_discovers_via_cwd_ancestor_walk(tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    _, projects = setup_run(\n        tmp_path, \"demo\", \"code\", _BODY_CODE, \"demo.code\", run_id=\"r2\", project=\"p\"\n    )\n    expected = _capture_cmd_next(\"p\", projects)\n\n    rc, out, err = _capture_hook(cwd=projects / \"p\", projects_root=projects)\n\n    assert rc == 0\n    assert err == \"\"\n    assert out == expected\n\n\ndef test_session_bound_resolution_wins(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch) -> None:\n    _, projects = setup_run(\n        tmp_path, \"demo\", \"code\", _BODY_CODE, \"demo.code\", run_id=\"r3\", project=\"p\"\n    )\n    expected = _capture_cmd_next(\"p\", projects)\n\n    write_identity(\"hook-test\")\n    sessions_dir().mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    session = Session(\n        id=generate_ulid(),\n        project=\"p\",\n        timeline=None,\n        run_id=\"r3\",\n        agent_id=\"hook-test\",\n        attached_at=\"2026-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\n        last_used_at=\"2026-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\n        role=\"writer\",\n    )\n    session.to_json(session_path(session.id))\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"ASTRID_SESSION_ID\", session.id)\n\n    # cwd is unrelated; session-bound discovery should still find slug 'p'.\n    unrelated = tmp_path / \"unrelated\"\n    unrelated.mkdir()\n    rc, out, err = _capture_hook(cwd=unrelated, projects_root=projects)\n    assert rc == 0\n    assert err == \"\"\n    assert out == expected\n\n\ndef test_silent_noop_when_cwd_has_no_run_and_no_session(tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    projects = tmp_path / \"projects\"\n    projects.mkdir()\n    cwd = tmp_path / \"elsewhere\"\n    cwd.mkdir()\n    (cwd / \"some_other_file.txt\").write_text(\"noise\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n    rc, out, err = _capture_hook(cwd=cwd, projects_root=projects)\n    assert rc == 0\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert err == \"\"\n"
```

```
1	"""Stable agent identity + first-run bootstrap.
2	
3	The identity record (``~/.astrid/identity.json``) is the per-machine agent
4	slug new sessions inherit by default. ``astrid attach --as agent:<id>``
5	overrides for a single tab without rewriting the file.
6	"""
7	
8	from __future__ import annotations
9	
10	import re
11	from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
12	from datetime import UTC, datetime
13	from typing import Any, Callable
14	
15	from astrid.core.project.jsonio import read_json, write_json_atomic
16	from astrid.core.session.paths import identity_path
17	
18	_AGENT_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}$")
19	
20	
21	class IdentityError(ValueError):
22	    """Raised when an agent identity record is malformed or input is invalid."""
23	
24	
25	def _now_iso() -> str:
26	    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")
27	
28	
29	@dataclass(frozen=True)
30	class Identity:
31	    agent_id: str
32	    created_at: str
33	
34	    @classmethod
35	    def from_dict(cls, raw: Any) -> "Identity":
36	        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
37	            raise IdentityError("identity record must be an object")
38	        agent_id = raw.get("agent_id")
39	        created_at = raw.get("created_at")
40	        if not isinstance(agent_id, str) or _AGENT_ID_RE.fullmatch(agent_id) is None:
41	            raise IdentityError(
42	                "identity.agent_id must be a slug (lowercase letters/digits/_-, "
43	                f"start with letter or digit), got {agent_id!r}"
44	            )
45	        if not isinstance(created_at, str) or not created_at:
46	            raise IdentityError("identity.created_at must be a non-empty string")
47	        return cls(agent_id=agent_id, created_at=created_at)
48	
49	
50	def read_identity() -> Identity | None:
51	    """Return the on-disk identity, or ``None`` when it does not exist."""
52	
53	    try:
54	        raw = read_json(identity_path())
55	    except FileNotFoundError:
56	        return None
57	    return Identity.from_dict(raw)
58	
59
```

```
55	
56	For each discovered slug (sorted), the hook re-prints `astrid next`
57	output (the prohibition preamble plus the current step) so Claude Code
58	re-injects it into the next turn. If no slugs are discovered the hook exits
59	silently with status 0 — your normal Claude Code sessions are unaffected.
60	
61	The hook does **not** require Claude's cwd to be the project state directory.
62	The projects-root scan means it works from any cwd, including a sibling repo
63	checkout where you actually edit code.
64
```
